Proof 1: Mendel's Law of Segregation

Statement: Mendel's Law of Segregation

Hypotheses: diploid organism, sexual reproduction

Conclusion: each gamete receives one allele per gene

Definitions: allele, gamete, segregation

Proof (Variation #1):

Step 1: During meiosis I, homologous chromosomes separate

Step 2: Each gamete gets one chromosome from each pair

Step 3: At fertilization, two alleles reunite

Step 4: 3:1 ratio in F2 confirms segregation

ID: 4ff065aaa3af | Status: UNFORMALIZED | Complexity: O(n)

Mutation test: Fails for linked genes or meiotic drive

Provenance: Variation #1 for theorem in domain Biology

Hypotheses: diploid organism, sexual reproduction

Conclusion: each gamete receives one allele per gene

Definitions: allele, gamete, segregation

Proof (Variation #184):

Step 1: During meiosis I, homologous chromosomes separate

Step 2: Each gamete gets one chromosome from each pair

Step 3: At fertilization, two alleles reunite

Step 4: 3:1 ratio in F2 confirms segregation
